Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the mechanics object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 13. The moduli space has dimension at most 621 with 3 connected components.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_13(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 13. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^621(S, F) is trivial. QED.
